PS C:\Users\penelope> $L = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
PS C:\Users\penelope>
PS C:\Users\penelope> function Find-Bytes([byte[]]$Pattern) {
>>     $Found = [System.Collections.Generic.List[int]]::new()
>>
>>     for ($i = 0; $i -le $B.Length - $Pattern.Length; $i++) {
>>         $Match = $true
>>
>>         for ($j = 0; $j -lt $Pattern.Length; $j++) {
>>             if ($B[$i + $j] -ne $Pattern[$j]) {
>>                 $Match = $false
>>                 break
>>             }
>>         }
>>
>>         if ($Match) {
>>             $Found.Add($i)
>>         }
>>     }
>>
>>     return $Found.ToArray()
>> }

>>     else {
>>         $L.Add("$($P.Name): " + (($Hits | ForEach-Object {

>>     }
>> }

PS C:\Users\penelope>
PS C:\Users\penelope> for ($i = 0; $i -lt $B.Length; $i++) {
>>     if ($B[$i] -ne 0 -and !$InBlock) {
>>         $Start = $i
>>         $InBlock = $true
>>     }
>>     elseif ($B[$i] -eq 0 -and $InBlock) {
>>         $Length = $i - $Start
>>
>>         if ($Length -ge 16) {
>>             $L.Add(("Start=0x{0:X8} Length={1}" -f $Start, $Length))
